// Symbols local to the field overlay

FldEntry = 0x005B0000; // type:func  overlay entry - registers the 8 FIELD program callbacks with m - 401160

FldDraw = 0x005B06A0; // type:func  program callback 4 (before frame-buffer effects) - sets views, draws every draw list, effects, menus, message windows, fades
